QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n We can launch CUDA kernels that compute candidate clusters for each unclustered seed in parallel. This is the dominant cost (O(N) seeds each O(N^2 * cluster size)). Then choose best on host.\n\nAlternative approach: compute all candidate clusters in parallel across seeds on GPU, each seed uses its own thread (or block) for its expansion. But the greedy expansion is sequential per seed, O(cluster_size) iterations each scanning N points with distance to all members O(cluster_size). That's a lot. Parallelize each candidate evaluation across points with a block, reducing max-dist per candidate. But per seed sequential iterations.\n\nGiven correctness requirement: exact same output as sequential. Floating point: distance sqrt. Need max_dist comparisons. Sequential uses std::max over members in order and compares with min_diameter strict <. Parallel reduction with different order could yield slightly different max but max is exact (no rounding, just selecting). So max over distances is exact regardless of order, since distances computed individually identical sqrt. Reduction max is exact. Then choose minimum over candidates. Tie-breaking must match sequential: sequential picks first candidate in ascending index with strictly smaller min_diameter. If GPU computes each candidate's max_dist identically, then host picks min with tie to smallest index. Fine. But per-seed candidate cluster greedy sequence must match. Each step picks candidate with min max_dist, strict <, and iterates ascending, so ties pick lowest index. GPU reduction to find argmin with tie -> lowest index. Good.\n\nBut there is a subtlety: sequential findClosestPoint computes max_dist over cluster members and requires max_dist < threshold and max_dist < min_diameter. It selects the minimum max_dist among all candidates satisfying < threshold. Since only candidates with max_dist < threshold considered, and min over them. Equivalent to find candidate with smallest max_dist, but only if that smallest < threshold. Actually points with max_dist >= threshold are excluded; the min over all candidates could be >= threshold -> -1. So GPU can compute min over all unclustered non-member candidates; if min < threshold pick it, else none. Same.\n\nSo candidate generation can be done with one CUDA kernel per expansion step across all seeds simultaneously: For each seed s (active), maintain members array. At each step, for each seed, evaluate all N candidates. That's O(N * N * members) per step but parallel. Could be quite heavy. Better: each seed handled by a CUDA block/warp and iterate internally. Let's think about performance and design.\n\nSimplest robust GPU implementation: flatten across seeds and candidates. Launch a kernel with grid = number of seeds * something, each thread handles one (seed, candidate) pair computing max distance over that seed's current members. Then reduce per seed to argmin. But member counts grow. Total work identical to sequential but massively parallel. However, number of steps equals max cluster size across seeds; kernels launched each step until all seeds done. Kernel launch overhead per step maybe fine.\n\nMemory: N seeds * N candidates doubles = N^2 * 8 bytes. For N=1000 => 8MB. For N=10000 => 800MB. Hmm could be large. We can avoid storing full N*N by per-seed block reduction writing only best per seed each step. Use thread blocks per seed where threads iterate candidates and reduce. Replace findClosestPoint/generateCandidateCluster/qtClustering with CUDA code.\n\nLet me draft CUDA portions.\n\n```\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n...\n#define MAX_THREADS 256\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double deviceDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n__device__ int runGreedy(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                         const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                         int seed, int N, double threshold,\n                         double* md, int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nt = blockDim.x;\n    __shared__ double s_val[MAX_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[MAX_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n        md[c] = clustered[c] ? DBL_MAX : deviceDistance(points[c], points[seed]);\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        md[seed] = DBL_MAX;\n        if (members_out) members_out[0] = seed;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < N) {\n        double best = DBL_MAX;\n        int best_idx = -1;\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v < best || (v == best && c < best_idx)) {\n                best = v;\n                best_idx = c;\n            }\n        }\n        s_val[tid] = best;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_idx;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int stride = nt >> 1; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const double v1 = s_val[tid];\n                const double v2 = s_val[tid + stride];\n                const int i1 = s_idx[tid];\n                const int i2 = s_idx[tid + stride];\n                if (v2 < v1 || (v2 == v1 && i2 < i1)) {\n                    s_val[tid] = v2;\n                    s_idx[tid] = i2;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = (s_val[0] < threshold) ? s_idx[0] : -1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int p = s_next;\n        if (p < 0) break;\n        ++count;\n        if (members_out && tid == 0) members_out[count - 1] = p;\n        if (tid == 0) md[p] = DBL_MAX;\n        __syncthreads();\n        // update\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += nt) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v < DBL_MAX) {\n                const double d = deviceDistance(points[c], points[p]);\n                md[c] = (d > v) ? d : v;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n```\n\nWait tie comparison when best_idx initialized -1: `(v == best && c < best_idx)` -> if v==DBL_MAX and best==DBL_MAX, c < -1 false. Good. If first finite v < INF true -> best_idx=c. Fine.\n\nBut after reduction, s_val[0]=DBL_MAX and s_idx[0]=-1 if all INF. Then s_next=-1. Good. If min finite but >= threshold, s_next=-1. Good.\n\nPotential race: `s_next` read after syncthreads, fine. Then thread0 sets md[p]. Other threads might start update loop based on p after syncthreads; we have syncthreads after md[p] write, so fine.\n\nMembers_out writes at index count-1 by thread0; no sync needed before return? Kernel end sync implicit. Host copies after kernel completion. But within loop, next iteration writes later index; fine.\n\nNow kernel wrappers:\n\n```\n__global__ void candidateSizesShared(\n    const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,\n    const int* seeds, int num_seeds, int N, double threshold,\n    int* sizes)\n{\n    extern __shared__ double smd[];\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        sizes[s] = runGreedy(points, clustered, seeds[s], N, threshold, smd, nullptr);\n    }\n}\n```\nHmm, gridDim.x may be num_seeds in shared path so loop one iteration. For shared path if we cap grid maybe multiple seeds; shared md reused correctly. But B for shared path could be num_seeds. Fine.\n\n```\n__global__ void candidateSizesGlobal(\n    ..., double* scratch, int* sizes)\n{\n    const size_t off = (size_t)blockIdx.x * (size_t)N;\n    double* md = scratch + off;\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x)\n        sizes[s] = runGreedy(..., md, nullptr);\n}\n```\n\nBut if grid B < num_seeds, blockIdx.x ranges 0..B-1, scratch indexed by blockIdx. Good.\n\nMembers kernels:\n```\n__global__ void candidateMembersShared(..., int seed, int N, double threshold,\n                                       int* members) {\n    extern __shared__ double smd[];\n    runGreedy(points, clustered, seed, N, threshold, smd, members);\n}\n__global__ void candidateMembersGlobal(..., double* md, int* members) {\n    runGreedy(..., md, members);\n}\n```\n\nNow host function qtClustering needs access to device resources. Let's implement:\n\n```\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold) {\n    const int N = (int)points.size();\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i=0;i<N;++i) unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Point* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_sizes = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    double* d_scratch = nullptr;\n    size_t scratch_blocks = 0;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(Point)*N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int)*N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_sizes, sizeof(int)*N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int)*N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(Point)*N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // device props shared\n    cudaDeviceProp prop; cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop,0);\n    size_t sharedBytes = (size_t)N * sizeof(double);\n    size_t maxShared = prop.sharedMemPerBlockOptin;\n    bool use_shared = sharedBytes <= maxShared;\n    if (use_shared) {\n       // set attribute for both kernels if sharedBytes > default 48K\n       cudaFuncSetAttribute(candidateSizesShared, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize, (int)std::min(sharedBytes, maxShared));\n       cudaFuncSetAttribute(candidateMembersShared, ...);\n    } else {\n       size_t bytesPerBlock = sharedBytes;\n       size_t budget = 2ull*1024*1024*1024;\n       size_t maxBlocks = budget / bytesPerBlock; if(maxBlocks<1)maxBlocks=1;\n       // cap maybe 65535*? grid x max large\n       scratch_blocks = std::min<size_t>((size_t)N, maxBlocks);\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratch, scratch_blocks * bytesPerBlock));\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned char> h_clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> h_sizes;\n    std::vector<int> h_members(N);\n\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n       const int num_seeds = (int)unclustered_indices.size();\n       // upload clustered + seeds\n       for (int i=0;i<N;++i) h_clustered[i] = clustered[i] ? 1 : 0;\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered, h_clustered.data(), N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds, unclustered_indices.data(), sizeof(int)*num_seeds, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n       if (use_shared) {\n           candidateSizesShared<<<num_seeds, MAX_THREADS, sharedBytes>>>(...);\n       } else {\n           int blocks = (int)std::min<size_t>((size_t)num_seeds, scratch_blocks);\n           candidateSizesGlobal<<<blocks, MAX_THREADS>>>(..., d_scratch, d_sizes);\n       }\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n       h_sizes.resize(num_seeds);\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_sizes.data(), d_sizes, sizeof(int)*num_seeds, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n       int best_i = -1; int best_size = -1;\n       for (int i=0;i<num_seeds;++i) {\n           if (h_sizes[i] > best_size) { best_size=h_sizes[i]; best_i=i; }\n       }\n       if (best_i < 0 || best_size <= 0) break;\n       const int best_seed = unclustered_indices[best_i];\n\n       // compute members\n       if (use_shared) {\n           candidateMembersShared<<<1, MAX_THREADS, sharedBytes>>>(d_points,d_clustered,best_seed,N,threshold,d_members);\n       } else {\n           candidateMembersGlobal<<<1, MAX_THREADS>>>(..., d_scratch, d_members);\n       }\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n       CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_members.data(), d_members, sizeof(int)*best_size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n       Cluster cl;\n       cl.seed_point = best_seed;\n       cl.members.assign(h_members.begin(), h_members.begin()+best_size);\n       clusters.push_back(std::move(cl));\n\n       for (int j=0;j<best_size;++j) {\n          int m = h_members[j];\n          clustered[m]=true;\n       }\n       unclustered_indices.erase(std::remove_if(...), end);\n    }\n\n    cudaFree...\n    return clusters;\n}\n```\n\nWait: For members global kernel use d_scratch as md for N doubles. d_scratch allocated scratch_blocks*bytesPerBlock where scratch_blocks>=1, so at least N doubles. Fine.\n\nLet's write code with templates.\n\n```\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                              \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,   \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            std::exit(1);                                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstatic const int QT_BLOCK_THREADS = 256;\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n```\n\nNote `distance` host function name; device different.\n\nrunGreedy template:\n\n```\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__device__ int greedyCandidate(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                               const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n                               const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                               int seed, int point_count, double threshold,\n                               double* md, int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n    __shared__ double s_val[QT_BLOCK_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[QT_BLOCK_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n        if (clustered[c]) {\n            md[c] = DBL_MAX;\n        } else if constexpr (USE_MATRIX) {\n            md[c] = dist_matrix[(size_t)seed * point_count + c];\n        } else {\n            md[c] = pointDistance(points[c], points[seed]);\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        md[seed] = DBL_MAX;\n        if (members_out) members_out[0] = seed;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < point_count) {\n        double best_value = DBL_MAX;\n        int best_index = -1;\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v < best_value || (v == best_value && c < best_index)) {\n                best_value = v;\n                best_index = c;\n            }\n        }\n        s_val[tid] = best_value;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_index;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int stride = nthreads >> 1; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const double v1 = s_val[tid];\n                const double v2 = s_val[tid + stride];\n                const int i1 = s_idx[tid];\n                const int i2 = s_idx[tid + stride];\n                if (v2 < v1 || (v2 == v1 && i2 < i1)) {\n                    s_val[tid] = v2;\n                    s_idx[tid] = i2;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = (s_val[0] < threshold) ? s_idx[0] : -1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int next = s_next;\n        if (next < 0) break;\n        ++count;\n        if (members_out && tid == 0) members_out[count - 1] = next;\n        if (tid == 0) md[next] = DBL_MAX;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n            const double current = md[c];\n            if (current < DBL_MAX) {\n                const double d = USE_MATRIX\n                    ? dist_matrix[(size_t)next * point_count + c]\n                    : pointDistance(points[c], points[next]);\n                md[c] = (d > current) ? d : current;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n    return count;\n}\n```\n\nCareful: `USE_MATRIX ? ... : pointDistance(...)` with template bool; compiler still compiles both sides? Point& b) {\n    const double dx = a.x - b.x;\n    const double dy = a.y - b.y;\n    return sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Greedily grow one candidate cluster.  For every not-yet-clustered point we\n// keep the largest distance to any member of the growing cluster (md).  The\n// next point added is the one with the smallest such value, provided it is\n// below the threshold.  This is exactly the sequential rule used by\n// findClosestPoint(), but the per-candidate maxima are maintained\n// incrementally so each expansion step costs O(point_count) instead of\n// O(point_count * cluster_size).\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__device__ int greedyCandidate(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                               const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n                               const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                               const int seed,\n                               const int point_count,\n                               const double threshold,\n                               double* md,\n                               int* __restrict__ members_out) {\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nthreads = blockDim.x;\n\n    __shared__ double s_val[QT_BLOCK_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[QT_BLOCK_THREADS];\n    __shared__ int s_next;\n\n    for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n        if (clustered[c]) {\n            md[c] = DBL_MAX;\n        } else if constexpr (USE_MATRIX) {\n            md[c] = dist_matrix[static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count + c];\n        } else {\n            md[c] = pointDistance(points[c], points[seed]);\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        md[seed] = DBL_MAX;\n        if (members_out) members_out[0] = seed;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    int count = 1;\n    while (count < point_count) {\n        // Per-thread minimum (ties resolve to the lowest index).\n        double best_value = DBL_MAX;\n        int best_index = -1;\n        for (int c = tid; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n            const double v = md[c];\n            if (v < best_value || (v == best_value && c < best_index)) {\n                best_value = v;\n                best_index = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        s_val[tid] = best_value;\n        s_idx[tid] = best_index;\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Block-wide minimum.  The comparison rule makes the result\n        // independent of the reduction order and matches the sequential\n        // first-minimum tie break.\n        for (int stride = nthreads >> 1; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < stride) {\n                const double v1 = s_val[tid];\n                const double v2 = s_val[tid + stride];\n                const int i1 = s_idx[tid];\n                const int i2 = s_idx[tid + stride];\n                if (v2 < v1 || (v2 == v1 && i2 < i1)) {\n                    s_val[tid] = v2;\n                    s_idx[tid] = i2;\n                }\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            s_next = (s_val[0] < threshold) ? s_idx[0] : -1;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int next = s_next;\n        if (next < 0) break; c < point_count; c += nthreads) {\n            const double current = md[c];\n            if (current < DBL_MAX) {\n                double d;\n                if constexpr (USE_MATRIX) {\n                    d = dist_matrix[static_cast<size_t>(next) * point_count + c];\n                } else {\n                    d = pointDistance(points[c], points[next]);\n                }\n                md[c] = (d > current) ? d : current;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    return count;\n}\n\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__global__ void candidateSizesSharedKernel(\n    const Point* __restrict__ points,\n    const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int num_seeds,\n    const int point_count,\n    const double threshold,\n    int* __restrict__ sizes) {\n    extern __shared__ double shared_md[];\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        sizes[s] = greedyCandidate<USE_MATRIX>(\n            points, dist_matrix, clustered, seeds[s], point_count, threshold,\n            shared_md, nullptr);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__global__ void candidateSizesGlobalKernel(\n    const Point* __restrict__ points,\n    const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int num_seeds,\n    const int point_count,\n    const double threshold,\n    double* __restrict__ scratch,\n    int* __restrict__ sizes) {\n    double* md = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * point_count;\n    for (int s = blockIdx.x; s < num_seeds; s += gridDim.x) {\n        sizes[s] = greedyCandidate<USE_MATRIX>(\n            points, dist_matrix, clustered, seeds[s], point_count, threshold,\n            md, nullptr);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__global__ void candidateMembersSharedKernel(\n    const Point* __restrict__ points,\n    const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int seed,\n    const int point_count,\n    const double threshold,\n    int* __restrict__ members) {\n    extern __shared__ double shared_md[];\n    greedyCandidate<USE_MATRIX>(points, dist_matrix, clustered, seed,\n                                point_count, threshold, shared_md, members);\n}\n\ntemplate <bool USE_MATRIX>\n__global__ void candidateMembersGlobalKernel(\n    const Point* __restrict__ points,\n    const double* __restrict__ dist_matrix,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int seed,\n    const int point_count,\n    const double threshold,\n    double* __restrict__ scratch,\n    int* __restrict__ members) {\n    greedyCandidate<USE_MATRIX>(points, dist_matrix, clustered, seed,\n                                point_count, threshold, scratch, members);\n}\n\n__global__ void buildDistanceMatrixKernel(const Point* __restrict__ points,\n                                          double* __restrict__ matrix,\n                                          const int point_count) {\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;\n    if (i < point_count && j < point_count) {\n        matrix[static_cast<size_t>(i) * point_count + j] =\n            pointDistance(points[i], points[j]);\n    }\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (GPU accelerated).  All seeds of every\n// clustering round are evaluated concurrently on the device; the host only\n// performs the reduction over candidate cardinalities and bookkeeping.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    Point* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;\n    int* d_sizes = nullptr;\n    int* d_members = nullptr;\n    double* d_scratch = nullptr;\n    double* d_dist_matrix = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, sizeof(Point) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, sizeof(unsigned char) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_sizes, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int) * N));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(), sizeof(Point) * N,\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    int device = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n\n    const size_t static_shared =\n        static_cast<size_t>(QT_BLOCK_THREADS) * sizeof(double) +\n        static_cast<size_t>(QT_BLOCK_THREADS) * sizeof(int) + sizeof(int);\n    const size_t shared_bytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double);\n    const size_t max_dynamic_shared =\n        prop.sharedMemPerBlockOptin > static_shared\n            ? For every not-yet-clustered point we\n104: // keep the largest distance to any member of the growing cluster (md).
